We also find that set framing, how the toolset presents the alternatives as competing or complementary, shifts the routing dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [LAPO: Leave-One-Turn Attribution for Self-Generated Process Rewards in Multi-Turn Search Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13501)
*Qiang Zhu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAPO提出一种基于向后留一归因的自生成过程监督方法，通过替换搜索回合为[DELETE]占位符并测量策略对正确答案的对数似然变化来估计回合贡献，无需额外奖励模型，在7个知识密集型QA数据集上平均EM达到0.326，优于IGPO基线0.053。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮搜索推理的强化学习通常仅依赖终端结果奖励，无法区分有用、冗余和有害的中间交互过程，缺乏细粒度的过程监督信号。

Method: 提出LAPO方法：1) 向后留一归因——对每轮搜索将其与检索观察替换为[DELETE]占位符；2) 计算答案似然增益——测量替换前后策略对正确答案的平均对数似然变化；3) 符号一致性门控——仅保留与原始归因分数方向一致的归一化过程优势。无需额外奖励模型、教师网络或验证器。

Result: 在7个知识密集型问答数据集上，LAPO的平均EM为0.326，超越最强步骤奖励基线IGPO达0.053。消融实验验证了向后归因和符号一致性门控具有互补优势。

Conclusion: 策略派生的回溯归因能够为多轮搜索智能体提供有效的过程监督信号，证明了无需外部监督即可实现细粒度过程奖励的方法可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-turn search reasoning typically relies on terminal outcome rewards, which cannot distinguish useful, redundant, and harmful intermediate interactions. We propose LAPO, a self-generated process-supervision method based on backward leave-one-turn attribution. For each search turn, LAPO replaces the turn and its retrieval observation with a fixed [DELETE] placeholder and measures the resulting change in the current policy's mean log-likelihood of the gold answer. This Answer-Likelihood Gain estimates the turn's contribution while preserving all downstream interactions, allowing early evidence to be evaluated in the complete reasoning context. LAPO further applies sign-consistency gating, retaining only normalized process advantages whose directions agree with their raw attribution scores. The method requires no additional reward model, teacher, verifier, or LLM-as-a-Judge. Across seven knowledge-intensive question-answering datasets with local retrieval, LAPO achieves an average exact-match score of 0.326, outperforming the strongest step-reward baseline, IGPO, by 0.053. Ablations show complementary benefits from backward attribution and sign-consistency gating, demonstrating that policy-derived retrospective attribution can provide effective process supervision for multi-turn search agents.

</details>


### [98] [How Far Can Root Cause Analysis Go on Real-World Telemetry Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13548)
*Athira Gopal,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化多智能体根因分析流水线（Structured Multi-Agent RCA pipeline），在OpenRCA基准上显著优于现有方法。通过逆向推理智能体分析失败原因，发现大多数故障中证据存在但未被正确推理（推理鸿沟），而非数据缺失。引入自动化规则挖掘减少人工知识依赖，并指出模型推理能力是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中微服务故障的根因分析需要处理大规模、多模态的遥测数据（指标、日志、追踪），现有经典方法和基于LLM的多智能体系统在此基准上准确率均较低，亟需更有效的方法。

Method: 提出结构化多智能体RCA流水线，支持领域知识驱动和无知识两种模式；设计逆向推理智能体，通过回溯正确答案所需的证据信号，将故障分类为推理鸿沟（证据存在但未被使用）或数据歧义（证据真正缺失）；引入自动化规则挖掘流水线，从逆向推理报告中系统提取判别规则。

Result: 结构化多智能体RCA流水线在OpenRCA基准上显著优于所有现有基线；逆向推理分析表明绝大多数故障属于推理鸿沟而非数据歧义；自动化规则挖掘减少了人工知识标注需求；模型推理能力和领域知识是性能的主要制约因素。

Conclusion: 根因分析的核心瓶颈不在于数据获取，而在于模型的推理能力；即使证据提取完全正确，推理性能仍存在实际边界；仅靠流水线工程和数据管道改进无法弥合这一差距，需要模型层面的进步。

Abstract: Identifying root causes in production microservice failures requires reasoning over large-scale, multimodal telemetry spanning metrics, logs, and traces, a problem that has proved resistant to both classical and LLM-based approaches. The OpenRCA dataset exemplifies these challenges: it is large-scale, multimodal, and lacks detailed domain knowledge, and yields consistently low accuracy across all existing methods. We show that classical causal discovery methods and existing LLM-based multi-agent systems fail to reliably identify root causes on this benchmark, and present a Structured Multi-Agent RCA pipeline that substantially outperforms existing LLM-based and classical baselines, supporting both domain-knowledge and knowledge-free operating modes. To diagnose where failures originate, we introduce a reverse reasoning agent that, given the correct answer, identifies which signals in the extracted anomalies support it and determines whether Stage~1 had access to those signals, classifying each failure as Reasoning Gap (evidence present but unused) or Data Ambiguity (evidence genuinely absent). This analysis reveals that the required evidence is present in the vast majority of failures: the bottleneck is not data access but the agent's ability to reason over it correctly. We further introduce an automated rule mining pipeline that systematically extracts discrimination rules from reverse reasoning reports, reducing reliance on manual knowledge curation. Across all configurations, model reasoning capability and domain knowledge are the primary constraints: stronger models embed more domain expertise, and explicit knowledge injection partially compensates for this gap. Reasoning performance remains practically bounded even when evidence extraction is perfect: scaffold engineering and better data pipelines alone cannot close this gap; progress requires improvements at the model level.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning with Tool-Augmented Evidence for Urban Region Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13558)
*Xixuan Hao,Yutian Jiang,Jiabo Liu,Yihang Yang,Guangyin Jin,Song Gao,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UrbanAgent 是一个将城市区域画像重新定义为推理驱动问题的多智能体框架，通过独立模态智能体的结构化协作推理和闭环证据获取，在碳排放、GDP和人口估计任务上平均提升8.1%的R²分数，并展现出强大的跨城市泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域画像方法主要依赖多模态表示学习，通过融合异构城市数据（如卫星图像、兴趣点、文本描述和3D建筑信息）进行预测。然而，这些方法本质上是相关性驱动的，假设跨模态一致性，且依赖静态流程，在异构或未见过的城市区域中鲁棒性有限。因此，需要一种更具推理能力和鲁棒性的框架。

Method: UrbanAgent 提出一种智能体框架：1) 为每种数据模态实例化独立智能体，进行结构化多智能体协作推理，明确处理跨模态不一致性而非将其吸收到单一表示中；2) 将指标预测扩展为主动证据获取和迭代推理的闭环过程，通过工具增强的外部知识检索来验证不确定推理，并利用强化学习优化。

Result: 在全球城市数据集上的碳排放、GDP和人口估计实验中，UrbanAgent 持续优于现有基线方法，平均R²提升8.1%，并在未见过城市场景中表现出强大的泛化性能。

Conclusion: UrbanAgent 通过推理驱动的多智能体协作框架有效解决了城市区域画像中跨模态不一致和静态流程的局限，展示了在异构城市环境中的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为城市计算提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Urban region profiling constitutes a core problem in urban computing, supporting applications such as population estimation, economic assessment, and environmental monitoring. Existing methods typically formulate this task as multimodal representation learning, fusing heterogeneous urban data, e.g., satellite imagery, points of interest, textual descriptions, and 3D building information, into latent embeddings for prediction. However, these approaches are largely correlation-driven, assume cross-modal consistency, and rely on static pipelines, which limit their robustness in heterogeneous or unseen urban regions. We propose UrbanAgent, an agentic framework that reframes urban region profiling as a reasoning-driven inference problem. UrbanAgent instantiates an independent agent for each data modality and performs structured multi-agent collaborative reasoning to explicitly address cross-modal inconsistencies rather than absorbing them into a single representation. In addition, UrbanAgent extends indicator prediction as a closed-loop process of active evidence acquisition and iterative reasoning, enabling agents to verify uncertain inferences through tool-augmented retrieval of external knowledge optimized via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on global urban datasets for Carbon emissions, GDP, and Population estimation show that UrbanAgent consistently outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average improvement of 8.1% in R2, and exhibiting strong generalization performance in unseen-city settings.

</details>


### [100] [AI advice suppresses people's willingness to say "I don't know", even when the advice is wrong and accuracy is incentivized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13562)
*Chiara Marcoccia,Walter Quattrociocchi,Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 即便AI建议是错误的，仅仅提供AI访问权限就会显著降低人们放弃回答的意愿，导致回答更多但正确率降低约三分之二，同时自信度却几乎翻倍。激励机制可以部分缓解这一现象，但无法完全恢复人们在无AI时的判断保留水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统（即使提供错误建议）如何影响人类判断中的元认知阈值——即人们决定自己是否知道足够多而可以回答问题的临界点。

Method: 五项实验（N=3,132），其中四项预注册，一项直接复制。参与者回答困难问题并始终可选择不回答。设计问题使AI建议为错误，从而将AI使用与准确率分离。

Result: AI访问权限几乎消除了参与者放弃判断的意愿（无论建议是主动请求还是被动展示）；参与者回答更多问题但正确率降低约三分之二，自信度却几乎翻倍；激励准确性和惩罚不准确性虽能减少寻求和遵循AI建议、提高准确率并增加放弃判断的频率，但仍远低于无AI条件。

Conclusion: 随着AI建议变得无处不在且可能未经请求就被提供，它们不仅影响答案准确性，还可能改变人们判断自己是否足够了解以回答问题的元认知阈值。

Abstract: Knowing when to say "I don't know" is fundamental to human judgment, yet AI assistants offer a fluent answer to almost any question. In five experiments (N = 3,132; four preregistered, one direct replication), participants answered difficult questions and could always decline to respond. We engineered the questions so that AI advice was wrong, separating AI use from its accuracy. Merely having access to AI nearly eliminated participants' willingness to suspend judgment, and this held whether the advice was actively requested or simply displayed. Consequently, participants answered more questions but were correct about a third as often as when AI was unavailable-yet their confidence nearly doubled. Incentivizing accuracy and penalizing inaccuracy led participants to seek and follow AI advice less, answer more accurately, and suspend judgment more often, though still far less than when AI was unavailable. As AI suggestions grow ubiquitous and unsolicited, they may not simply affect answer accuracy; they may even alter the metacognitive threshold at which people decide whether they know enough to answer.

</details>


### [101] [SAFETY SENTRY: Context-Aware Human Intervention via EXECUTE-ASK-REFUSE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13594)
*Tianyu Chen,Chujia Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safety Sentry，一种轻量级防护模型，将LLM代理的工具调用安全判定从二元分类重构为{执行、询问、拒绝}三路路由决策，通过单次解码调用实现实例级判断，并支持单一阈值调整以适应不同风险容忍度，在准确率和安全召回率上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二元安全防护模型将"动作本身有害"与"动作在上下文中不适当"两种不同决策混为一谈，且基于动作类别而非实例粒度进行判断，导致频繁中断用户操作、削弱自主性，并使用户对高风险的警报习惯性放行。

Method: 将问题重构为每个实例的三路路由决策{EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE}，实现为轻量级防护模型Safety Sentry，推理过程简化为单次解码调用，通过单一解码阈值使固定检查点无需重新训练即可适配不同风险容忍度的部署场景。

Result: Safety Sentry在总体准确率和安全相关召回率上超过多种开源权重模型和闭源前沿基线，同时能够双向控制错误率。

Conclusion: 实例级三路路由决策比二元分类更适合作为LLM代理工具调用的安全防护机制，Safety Sentry以轻量高效的方式实现了更精细的安全控制。

Abstract: LLM agents act on real-world environments through tool calls, and a single misjudged action can cause irreversible harm. The standard safeguard is a guard model that labels each proposed action as safe or unsafe, but this binary view conflates two distinct decisions: whether the action is harmful in itself, and whether it is appropriate given the user's context. It also operates at the granularity of action categories rather than individual instances, producing routine interruptions that erode autonomy and train users to wave through the most consequential alerts. We reframe the problem as a per-instance three-way routing decision over {EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE} and instantiate it with Safety Sentry, a lightweight guard model whose inference reduces to a single decoding call. A single decoding-time threshold lets one fixed checkpoint be re-positioned across deployments of differing risk tolerance without retraining. Safety Sentry outperforms a broad set of open-weight and frontier closed-source baselines on overall accuracy and safety-related recall, while controlling both directional error rates simultaneously.

</details>


### [102] [Automatic Ordinary Differential Equations Discovery For Biological Systems Using Large Language Model Powered Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13608)
*David Krongauz,Arad Zulti,Eran Segal,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDA系统，结合大语言模型和符号回归，自动发现生物动力系统的常微分方程模型，在检索、外推和开放式发现任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科学发现是计算学者的长期目标，现有方法要么聚焦狭窄的方程发现基准，要么关注端到端自动化流程，而生物系统的自动建模探索不足。

Method: MEDA系统由LLM和符号回归驱动，包含知识检索、变量定义、机械约束生成、候选ODE提议、模型拟合与评估等模块，形成完整的智能体框架。

Result: 在规范模型检索、推理外推和开放式发现任务中，MEDA正确恢复状态变量，实现强结构恢复，并生成生物学合理的发现导向模型。消融实验表明知识引导形式化和机械约束是关键组件。

Conclusion: MEDA实现了对生物动力系统的自动化ODE发现，但数值拟合可能保留轨迹兼容但生物学不正确的方程，需结合知识约束才能确保模型合理性。

Abstract: Automatic scientific discovery has long been a goal of computational scholars - a machine that can discover nature's secrets on its own, moving computational systems beyond data-fitting tools toward the generation and refinement of mechanistic models of the universe. Recent advances in symbolic regression (SR) and large-language-model (LLM)-based agents suggest that such systems can recover equations from data, incorporate domain priors, and automate parts of the research workflow. However, most existing approaches either focus on narrow equation-discovery benchmarks or broad end-to-end automation pipelines, while biological systems remain comparatively underexplored. Here, we introduce the MEDA system, an LLM- and SR-powered agentic framework for discovering ordinary-differential-equation (ODE) models of biological and biologically inspired dynamical systems. MEDA retrieves background knowledge, defines admissible variables, generates mechanistic constraints, proposes candidate ODEs, and fits and evaluates them. We evaluate it across canonical model retrieval, reasoning-based extrapolation to unseen variants, and open-ended discovery, with and without experimental data. Across these settings, MEDA recovered the correct state variables, achieved strong structural recovery in retrieval and extrapolation tasks, and produced biologically plausible discovery-oriented models. Ablation and robustness analyses show that knowledge-guided formalization and mechanistic constraints are load-bearing components, whereas numerical fitting alone can preserve trajectory-compatible but biologically incorrect equations.

</details>


### [103] [STOCKTAKE: Measuring the Gap Between Perception and Action in LLM Agents with a Fair Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13618)
*Sagar Deb,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STOCKTAKE是一个26周供应链补货基准测试，基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程构建，包含六个隐藏因子过程。它能将智能体失败分离为状态估计错误和控制错误（知行差距）。实验表明，四个前沿LLM智能体可检测84-88%的隐藏故障，但技能得分从0.62到-0.23不等，两个模型低于症状盲点基线。失败表现为两面：诊断正确后仍缺货（34-43%），以及过度响应导致成本超过保护收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估无法区分"误读世界"和"正确读世界但未能行动"两种失败模式，因为参考策略要么读取了智能体看不到的特权信息，要么根本不存在。需要一种能分别测量状态估计和控制能力的评估框架。

Method: 构建STOCKTAKE基准：26周供应链补货POMDP，含六个隐藏因子过程。设计公平参考策略（oracle）：每个因子的精确贝叶斯滤波器驱动rollout策略，使用与智能体相同的观测流。评分机制：症状盲点基线(0)到oracle(1)的skill score衡量综合表现；每周书面推理分析得到stated-belief detection lag（状态估计延迟）和knowing-doing rate（知行率），分离状态估计与控制的测量。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, Grok 4.5四模型能检测84-88%的隐藏故障，通常在故障发生后一周内检测到。但skill score跨度从0.62到-0.23，其中两个模型低于症状盲点基线，尽管它们命名因子的速度略快于跑赢基线的两个模型。在持续压力下，每个模型34-43%的诊断正确周仍出现缺货。低于基线的两个模型在诊断周缺货率最低，说明响应不足只是知行差距的一面，另一面是响应成本超过保护收益。

Conclusion: STOCKTAKE成功分离并测量了LLM智能体在部分可观测决策任务中的两种失败模式：状态估计失败和控制（知行）失败。知行差距表现为两个方向：响应不足导致缺货，以及过度响应导致成本超过保护收益。该基准为评估长期决策智能体提供了更细致的分析工具。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly evaluated on multi-week decision tasks in which the state that drives cost is never directly observed. On such tasks the final cost cannot say why an agent failed: it may have misread the world, or read it correctly and still failed to act (the knowing-doing gap). Existing evaluations cannot separate these two failures; their reference policies either read privileged information the agent never sees, or are missing altogether. We introduce STOCKTAKE, a 26-week supply-chain replenishment benchmark built as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with six hidden factor processes, designed so that a fair reference policy is computable: an exact Bayes filter per factor drives a rollout policy on the identical observation stream the agent receives. Scoring each run between a symptom-blind base-stock floor (0) and this oracle (1) yields a skill score, and grading each week's written rationale yields a stated-belief detection lag and a knowing-doing rate, so state estimation and control are measured separately. On fifty seeds with curated stress profiles, Claude Sonnet 5, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Grok 4.5 detect 84-88% of hidden failures, typically within a week of onset, yet span skill scores from 0.62 to -0.23: two of the four end below the symptom-blind floor while naming factors slightly faster than the two that beat it. The failure has two faces. Where stress persists, 34-43% of correctly diagnosed stress weeks still end in stockout for every model, a rate that partly reflects the severity of the weeks models notice. That rate also runs opposite to skill: the two models under the floor stock out least on diagnosed weeks, so under-response is only one face of the gap, and their traces point to the other, responses whose cost exceeds what they protect. STOCKTAKE measures both directions of that failure.

</details>


### [104] [UESF-Bench: Benchmarking and Probing for Unified Embodied Seeking and Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13621)
*Kun Yu,Jianhua Yang,Yixiang Chen,Changwei Wang,Hongyuan Yu,Yan Huang,Fushuo Huo,Ya Jing,Zhumin Chen,Keji He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UESF-Bench统一基准和SeekFollow-VLA框架，解决具身智能体在动态环境中先搜索语言描述目标再持续跟随的问题，相比现有基线方法在单人及多人场景下均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导人类跟随基准假设初始时目标可见，忽略了智能体需要先搜索目标再在动态环境中持续跟随的现实需求；同时搜索和跟随通常被作为分离任务处理，缺乏统一的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建大规模多样化统一基准UESF-Bench；提出SeekFollow-VLA视觉-语言-动作框架，采用任务驱动路由机制实现搜索与跟随之间的隐式阶段推理和状态转换建模。

Result: SeekFollow-VLA在单人及多人生环境中均显著优于单头（single-head）和双头（dual-head）基线方法。

Conclusion: UESF-Bench为统一具身搜索与跟随提供了标准评估平台，SeekFollow-VLA为该任务建立了有效基线，验证了任务驱动路由机制在搜索-跟随联合任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Language-guided human following is an important capability for embodied agents, but existing benchmarks typically assume that the target person is visible at the start of an episode. This setting simplifies the problem and overlooks a more realistic requirement: an agent often needs to first find a language-described target and then persistently follow that target in a dynamic environment. While recent work has started to study human search, existing settings are typically evaluated in task-specific scenarios and often rely on stronger prior knowledge of the environment. Moreover, they usually treat searching and following as separate tasks and still lack a unified benchmark for systematic evaluation. To address these limitations, we introduce the Unified Embodied Seeking and Following Benchmark (UESF-Bench), a large-scale and diverse benchmark for embodied human seeking and following. The benchmark requires agents to handle semantic-guided exploration, reliable behavior switching and recovery, and delayed identity grounding. To this end, we propose SeekFollow-VLA, a vision-language-action framework with a task-driven routing mechanism for latent phase inference and transition modeling between seeking and following. Experimental results show that SeekFollow-VLA achieves clear improvements over both single-head and dual-head baselines across single-person and multi-person environments, establishing a baseline for unified embodied seek-and-follow.

</details>


### [105] [Explaining Reinforcement Learning Agents via Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13655)
*Celeste Veronese,Edoardo Zorzi,Daniele Meli,Alessandro Farinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为解决可解释强化学习（XRL）缺乏客观评估指标的问题，提出了基于归纳逻辑编程（ILP）的符号化策略提取方法，并定义了激活率、特征覆盖度、句法距离和语义距离四类可解释性度量，用于量化逻辑规则与智能体行为之间的对齐程度、特征作用及策略演化，实验验证了这些度量在单/多智能体RL中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XRL大多依赖用户研究，缺乏共享的客观评估指标；而基于逻辑的XAI方法虽能提供可读的抽象，但缺乏对逻辑表示可解释程度的系统性量化方法。

Method: 采用ILP从RL策略中提取符号化逻辑规则，定义激活率、特征覆盖度、句法距离和语义距离四类可解释性度量，分别量化符号规则与智能体行为的对齐程度、特征在决策中的作用、策略在训练过程中的演化以及多智能体间的协调模式。

Result: 实验表明：所提度量能揭示超越全局回报的动作级学习动态；提供比传统全局特征重要性方法更细粒度的特征洞察；揭示多智能体RL中的协调、专业化与适应模式；为动作特定策略的迁移与泛化提供关键见解。

Conclusion: 该工作一方面推进了XRL的客观评估，另一方面为逻辑XAI提供了量化逻辑规则可解释性的原理性方法，超越了常识性评估和简单命题逻辑片段。

Abstract: Explainable Reinforcement Learning (XRL) seeks to make Reinforcement Learning (RL) policies more transparent and interpretable, a key requirement in safety-critical and human-centric scenarios. However, it is mostly based on user studies, thus targeting the needs of a specific audience and lacking shared evaluation metrics. On the other hand, logic-based approaches within eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) provide compact, human-readable abstractions of decision-making. However, the systematic quantification of the explainability degree of logical representations remains an open problem. This work aims to advance the state of the art in XRL by introducing objective and planning-oriented metrics for policy explainability in RL settings. At the same time, it contributes to the field of logic for XAI by providing a principled way to quantify the explainability of logical rules, moving beyond common-sense assessments and simple propositional fragments. We employ Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) to extract symbolic representations of RL policies and define a novel set of explainability metrics, including activation rate, feature coverage, syntactic distance and semantic distance. These metrics quantify alignment between symbolic rules and agent behavior, the role of features in decision-making, and the evolution of policies during training and across agents in single and multi-agent RL. Experiments across different RL domains show that the proposed metrics highlight action-specific learning dynamics beyond global return, provide fine-grained insights into domain features beyond classical approaches for global feature importance estimation, and uncover coordination, specialization, and adaptation patterns in MARL. Moreover, they provide crucial insights for the transfer and generalization of action-specific policies.

</details>


### [106] [When Bots Join the Team: Bot Adoption and the Institutional Fabric of Open-Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13679)
*Yongren Shi,Wenyi Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI机器人加入开源软件团队后对组织协调的影响。通过分析2,991个GitHub项目在采用首个机器人前后两年的数据，发现机器人加入后，重复协作增加、机器人被识别度提高、冲突级联减少、输出独特性增强。这些变化集中在采用时间点附近。研究认为可预测的规则型智能体可以成为社区社会基础设施的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当自动化智能体成为团队常规参与者时，群体组织是增强还是削弱？这是一个基础性问题。现有研究缺乏对机器人作为参与者而非工具的大规模实证分析。

Method: 以开源软件为研究场景，将机器人为视为参与者而非工具，分析2,991个GitHub项目在采用首个机器人前后各两年的数据。测量制度理论中与持久协调相关的三种能力（重复参与、社会记忆、角色分化）和两种结果（冲突级联、输出独特性）。

Result: 机器人采用后观察到：更多重复协作、机器人被识别度提升、冲突级联减少、输出独特性增强。这些变化集中在采用时间点附近，而非逐渐积累。能力按功能（协调vs分化）而非按提供者（人类vs机器人）预测结果；人类侧能力解释了机器人-冲突关联，但未解释机器人-独特性关联。

Conclusion: 可预测、基于规则的智能体可以成为社区社会基础设施的一部分。机器人是契机，社会组织是机制。

Abstract: AI agents are joining human teams, raising a basic question: when an automated agent becomes a regular participant, does group organization strengthen or weaken? We study this question in open-source software, where bots open pull requests, review code, and merge changes alongside people, leaving a public record of every interaction. Treating bots as participants rather than tools, we examine 2,991 GitHub projects for two years before and after each adopted its first bot. We measure three capabilities that institutional theory links to durable coordination - repeated engagement, social memory, and role differentiation - and two outcomes: conflict cascades and output distinctiveness. Bot adoption is followed by more repeated collaboration, greater recognition of specific bots in discussion, fewer conflict cascades, and more distinctive outputs. These changes cluster around adoption rather than accumulating gradually. Because we lack an untreated comparison group, we interpret the results as precisely timed associations, not causal effects. Two patterns are difficult for alternative explanations to account for: capabilities predict outcomes according to their function - coordination versus differentiation - rather than whether humans or bots provide them, and human-side capabilities account for the bot-conflict association but not the bot-distinctiveness association. The findings are consistent with a specific interpretation: predictable, rule-based agents can become part of a community's social infrastructure. The bot is the occasion; social organization is the mechanism.

</details>


### [107] [AgentCompass: A Unified Evaluation Infrastructure for Agent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13705)
*Zichen Ding,Jiaye Ge,Shufan Jiang,Kai Chen,Mo Li,Qingqiu Li,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tiaohao Liang,Shudong Liu,Zerun Ma,Zixing Shang,Wenhui Tian,Zun Wang,Liwei Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Qi Zhang,Songyang Zhang,Peiheng Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCompass是一个开源、轻量级、可扩展的LLM智能体评估基础设施，通过解耦基准测试、执行引擎和环境三大组件，支持20+基准测试和轨迹分析，解决了现有评估流程碎片化和耦合度高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的评估流程高度碎片化且耦合紧密，导致结果难以复现且存在大量重复工程工作，亟需统一、可扩展的评估基础设施。

Method: 将评估过程解耦为Benchmark（基准测试）、Harness（执行引擎）和Environment（环境）三个独立组件；采用容错异步运行时；提供轨迹分析工具用于诊断奖励破解等细粒度失败模式。

Result: 原生支持5个能力维度的20+基准测试，实现了灵活配置、可复现和可扩展的评估基础设施。

Conclusion: AgentCompass为智能体研究提供了可扩展、可复现的统一评估基础设施，有望推动该领域的发展和标准化。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, the need for unified evaluation infrastructure becomes critical. However, current evaluation pipelines remain highly fragmented and tightly coupled, hindering reproducibility and causing redundant engineering. To address this, we introduce AgentCompass, an open-source, lightweight, and extensible infrastructure for evaluating LLM-based agents. AgentCompass organizes the evaluation process around three independent components, namely Benchmark, Harness, and Environment, thereby enabling flexible configurations without requiring the reimplementation of complex execution logic. Furthermore, it features a fault-tolerant asynchronous runtime and comprehensive trajectory analysis tools to transparently diagnose nuanced failure modes like reward-hacking. Natively supporting over 20 benchmarks across five capability dimensions, AgentCompass provides the community with a scalable and reproducible infrastructure for advancing agent research.

</details>


### [108] [CAVA: Canonical Action Verification and Attestation for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13716)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAVA是一个运行时语义层，将异构的代理AI系统活动转化为规范的运行时动作对象，解决多运行时环境下动作审批、执行证据绑定和独立验证的治理难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI系统通过异构运行时（本地编码钩子、SDK工具、浏览器自动化、托管代理轨迹、API网关、工作流引擎）执行操作，同一操作（如发布代码、转移资金、导出数据）在不同运行时中记录格式互不兼容，导致难以回答"实际批准了什么动作、什么证据将批准与执行绑定、独立验证者能否复现同一动作身份"等基本治理问题。

Method: CAVA定义规范动作身份、语义模式检测、批准绑定、收据完整性、运行时可移植投影和可选证明基板。CAVA位于Proof-Carrying Agent Actions (PCAA)之下：PCAA定义部署者拥有的路由-审查-证明治理流程，CAVA定义该流程治理的稳定动作对象。

Result: 通过96个种子、384个变体的基准测试，覆盖语义等价性、语义分离、包装器绕过、假阳性控制、批准绑定、收据可复现性、证明篡改检测、运行时可移植性、语义模式检测、策略退化和Azure部署演练等维度。

Conclusion: 提出动作级规范化与策略可寻址语义模式是部署方AI治理的必要基础，贡献在于系统化地构建了异构运行时环境下的规范动作表示框架。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly act through heterogeneous runtimes: local coding hooks, SDK tools, browser automation, managed-agent traces, API gateways, and workflow engines. A single operational act such as publishing code, changing identity state, moving money, or exporting data may therefore be represented by many incompatible runtime records. This makes a basic governance question difficult to answer: what action was actually approved, what evidence binds the approval to execution, and can an independent verifier reproduce the same action identity later?
  This paper presents Canonical Action Verification and Attestation (CAVA), a runtime-semantics layer for converting heterogeneous agent activity into canonical runtime action objects. CAVA is positioned below Proof-Carrying Agent Actions (PCAA): PCAA defines the deployer-owned route-review-prove governance process, while CAVA defines the stable action object that process governs. The paper formalizes canonical action identity, semantic pattern detection, approval binding, receipt integrity, runtime-portable projection, and optional attestation substrates. We study a reference implementation through a 96-seed, 384-variant benchmark covering semantic equivalence, semantic separation, wrapper bypass, false-positive control, approval binding, receipt reproducibility, attestation tamper detection, runtime portability, semantic pattern detection, policy degradation, and Azure deployment drills. The contribution is a systems formulation of action-level canonicalization and policy-addressable semantic patterns as a necessary substrate for deployer-side AI governance.

</details>


### [109] [Experience Memory Graph: One-Shot Error Correction for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13884)
*Wenjun Wang,Yuchen Fang,Fengrui Liu,Zibo Liang,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Memory Graph (EMG)框架，将LLM智能体的失败恢复问题转化为图匹配问题，通过构建动作决策图并提取成功子图与编辑路径来指导智能体单次无循环执行，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上优于现有反思基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在复杂长程任务中容易出现累积错误且难以从失败中恢复；已有的自纠正机制依赖基于提示的反思，存在脆弱性、高时延与API成本、任务特定记忆难以泛化等问题。

Method: EMG框架：1) 将失败探索轨迹与成功专家轨迹转化为有向动作决策图；2) 通过图匹配提取公共子图（成功工作流）和图编辑路径（指示如何增删改动作以纠正错误）；3) 构建包含任务内节点和跨任务边的记忆图；4) 测试时检索相关经验，引导智能体单次无循环执行。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld基准上，EMG在成功率和平均奖励方面一致优于最先进的反思基线方法，且无需测试时的试错循环。

Conclusion: EMG通过图匹配和记忆图结构，有效解决了LLM智能体在长程任务中失败恢复问题，实现了高效、可泛化的错误纠正，避免了传统反思方法的固有缺陷。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown remarkable capabilities in autonomous decision-making by generating sequential trajectories of states, actions, and observations. However, in complex, long-horizon tasks, these agents frequently suffer from compounding errors and struggle to recover from failures. Existing self-correction mechanisms rely on prompt-based reflection, which is inherently brittle, incurs heavy time and API costs due to iterative trial-and-error loops, and produces task-specific memory that may be hard to generalize to new scenarios. To address this, we propose Experience Memory Graph (EMG), a framework that reformulates agent failure recovery as a graph matching problem. At training time, we convert both failed exploration trajectories and successful expert trajectories into directed action decision graphs. By matching these graphs, we extract common subgraphs (successful workflows) and graph edit paths that explicitly indicate how to correct failures (e.g., which actions to add, delete, or relabel under a given observation), and store them in a memory graph with intra-task nodes and cross-task edges. At test time, EMG retrieves relevant insights and guides the agent in a single, loop-free execution. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that EMG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art reflection baselines in success rate and average reward, while requiring no test-time trial-and-error.

</details>


### [110] [AIMO Interpretability Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13899)
*Michal Štefánik,Philipp Mondorf,Andreas Waldis,Qianying Liu,Chuan Yang,Michal Spiegel,Josef Kuchař,Marek Kadlčík,Adam Vawda-Oomerjee,Chaoran Liu,Simon Frieder,Barbara Plank,Fazl Barez,Pontus Stenetorp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIMO可解释性挑战赛，旨在通过模型内部机制区分前沿数学语言模型的稳健推理与虚假推理，比赛提供奥林匹克级数学问题、前沿推理模型访问及对抗鲁棒性评估，最终建立开放鲁棒性基准，连接可解释性与泛化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推理基准仅依赖最终答案准确率，无法区分模型是依赖稳定的推理机制还是脆弱的推理捷径，存在根本局限性。

Method: 构建基于AIMO问题的奥林匹克级数学推理题集及其符号表示以生成新功能变体，提供前沿推理模型访问权限，评估模型在这些问题上的对抗鲁棒性，并借助计算基础设施支持参与者开发识别模型稳健推理的方法。

Result: 创建新的开放鲁棒性基准和基线系统，为数学推理与可解释性提供标准化测评基础。

Conclusion: 该比赛将可解释性与泛化研究联系起来，围绕AI决策是否可泛化（从而可靠）这一核心问题展开，旨在判断前沿AI模型决策在多大程度上是可泛化的。

Abstract: We propose the AIMO Interpretability Challenge, a competition on distinguishing robust from spurious reasoning in frontier mathematical language models based on the models' internal mechanisms. The challenge is motivated by a central limitation of standard reasoning benchmarks: strong final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a model relies on stable reasoning mechanisms or exploits brittle reasoning shortcuts. Building on AI Mathematical Olympiad (AIMO) problems and submissions, together with resources from the Fields Model Initiative, the competition will provide (1) newly-published olympiad-level math reasoning problems and their symbolic representations, allowing generation of novel functional variants, (2) access to frontier reasoning models, and (3) assessments of models' adversarial robustness on these problems. Participants will use these resources, along with our computing infrastructure support, to develop methods for identifying which models solve problems robustly. Our competition will also create a new, open robustness benchmark and baseline systems, aiming to provide a lasting foundation for standard benchmarking in mathematical reasoning and interpretability. Scientifically, the competition connects interpretability and generalization research around a central question in AI research: can we determine if, and to what extent, the decision-making of frontier AI models is generalizable and thus, reliable?

</details>


### [111] [A Self-Evolving Agent for Longitudinal Personal Health Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13940)
*Haoran Li,Jiebi Deng,Tong Jin,Jinghong Han,Yuxin Wang,Zexin Wang,Qingyi Si,Weikang Gong,Xiahai Zhuang,Jia You,Wei Cheng,Jianfeng Feng,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HealthClaw是一个开源智能体架构，通过分离安全规则与记忆模块、利用归纳机制实现自我进化，在纵向健康管理任务中将准确率从0.2%提升至45.7%，同时降低隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康AI系统将每次请求独立处理，无法记忆和适应用户随时间变化的习惯、偏好、测量值和风险，缺乏持续学习能力。

Method: 提出HealthClaw架构：将共享安全规则/医学知识与私有纵向记忆（个人概况、可复用流程、事件轨迹）分离，每次交互后通过归纳机制决定更新概况、修订流程、保留为事件或排除。

Result: 在900个纵向支持探针中准确率从0.2%提升至45.7%，上下文暴露降低71.7%；100个隐私探针中隐私感知质量更高、不安全披露更少；9个生物医学任务中主要指标平均绝对增益27个百分点，7个经FDR校正仍显著。

Conclusion: HealthClaw验证了受管控的自我进化记忆对纵向个人健康智能体的有效性，但临床效果需前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Personal health management unfolds over repeated encounters, yet most health AI systems treat each request in isolation. We developed HealthClaw, an open-source agent architecture that updates support as a person's routines, preferences, measurements and risks change. It separates shared safety rules and medical knowledge from private longitudinal memory containing profile facts, reusable procedures and episodic traces. After each episode, induction determines what should update the profile, revise a procedure, remain episodic or be excluded. We evaluated HealthClaw with a synthetic year-long benchmark and nine 200-case biomedical tasks. Across 900 longitudinal support probes, answer accuracy increased from 0.2% with current-query prompting to 45.7% with HealthClaw, while prompt-side context exposure was 71.7% lower than with full-history prompting. In 100 privacy probes, HealthClaw produced higher privacy-aware answer quality and fewer unsafe disclosures than both baselines. Across the biomedical tasks, the mean absolute gain in the task-specific primary metric was 27.0 percentage points, and seven gains remained significant after false-discovery-rate correction. These offline benchmarks support governed, self-evolving memory for longitudinal personal health agents, although clinical effectiveness requires prospective evaluation. HealthClaw is publicly available at https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw.

</details>


### [112] [Do Agent Optimizers Compound? A Continual-Learning Evaluation on Terminal-Bench 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14004)
*Wenxiao Wang,Priyatham Kattakinda,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体优化方法的复合增益问题，发现只有在优化循环中内置回归控制（防止短视捷径解）时，优化收益才能在持续学习场景中累积。在Terminal-Bench 2.0的两阶段持续学习评估中，RELAI-VCL方法通过内置回归控制，在所有评估阶段均达到最高通过率（终身平均76.4%），而GEPA和Meta Harness则分别因负迁移和无法继续改进而表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体优化方法大多仅报告单次静态基准下的改进，但这无法反映实际部署场景中优化被递归应用（新任务、新失败不断出现）的情况。核心问题是：优化驱动的增益能否复合累积？即经过一次优化后，在新任务上再次优化是否会侵蚀第一轮收益。

Method: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0的困难任务上构建两阶段持续学习评估框架，比较三种智能体优化方法（GEPA、Meta Harness、RELAI-VCL），在相同优化预算下，先进行常规单阶段优化，再引入新任务进行第二阶段优化，评估各方法的迁移能力和持续改进能力。

Result: 所有方法在单阶段静态设置中均优于基线。但引入新任务后出现分化：GEPA优化后的智能体出现负迁移（低于未优化基线）；Meta Harness正迁移良好但无法在第二轮优化中继续改进；RELAI-VCL是唯一既能正迁移到未见任务，又能在新任务加入优化目标后继续提升的方法，在所有阶段的通过率最高，终身平均通过率达76.4%（对比GEPA 66.0%、Meta Harness 64.6%、基线58.7%）。

Conclusion: 优化收益复合累积的关键在于优化循环中内置回归控制，这提供了防止无法泛化的捷径解法的归纳偏置。只有具备这种机制的方法才能实现持续学习场景下的增益累积。

Abstract: Most reported gains from agent-optimization methods are one-shot: an agent is optimized against a fixed benchmark and the resulting improvement is reported as if it were a stable property of the method. This does not test the setting that matters for deployed agents, where optimization is applied recursively as new failures and new tasks appear over time. The central question this raises is whether optimizer-driven gains compound: after an agent has been optimized once, can it be optimized again on newly arrived tasks without eroding the gains the first round produced? We study this question with a two-phase continual-learning evaluation built from hard tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.0, comparing three approaches to agent-harness optimization (GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI's Verifiable Continual Learning, RELAI-VCL) under identical optimization budgets. All three methods improve over the baseline agent in the conventional, static, single-phase setting. However, once new tasks are introduced, the methods diverge sharply: GEPA's optimized agent transfers below the unoptimized baseline, Meta Harness transfers well but fails to improve further once given a second optimization budget, and RELAI-VCL is the only method that both transfers positively to unseen tasks and continues improving after those tasks are folded into the optimization objective, reaching the highest pass rate at every evaluated stage and the highest lifelong average pass rate overall (76.4% vs. 66.0% for GEPA, 64.6% for Meta Harness, and 58.7% for the baseline). Our key observation was that optimization gains compounded only when regression control was built into the optimization loop, providing an inductive bias against shortcut solutions that fail to generalize.

</details>


### [113] [AI-accelerated End-to-End Framework for Rapid Professional Upskilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14044)
*Tam Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Robert Ogburn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的AI加速技能提升框架，覆盖知识获取、内容开发、审查与验证、教学和评估开发五个阶段，并通过美国NASBA认证、学员快速通过NVIDIA认证考试以及生成1,267项风险数据集三个外部信号验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到2030年，每100名工人中有59人需要技能重塑或提升，而企业技能差距的关闭时间从2014年的约3天增长到2018年的36天。现有框架大多只加速技能提升计划的单个阶段，且普遍缺乏行业验证，亟需一个经过验证的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个端到端的AI加速框架，涵盖知识获取、内容开发、内容审查与验证、教学和评估开发五个阶段，同时注重生产效率和学习效率。

Result: 该框架通过了三个外部信号验证：(1)美国NASBA批准基于该框架的技能提升计划可获得继续专业教育学分；(2)3名学员在极短时间内通过NVIDIA认证专业Agentic AI考试，另有14人正在学习；(3)该计划的知识库支持生成包含1,267项风险条目的多智能体AI系统风险管理数据集。

Conclusion: 该端到端AI加速框架通过行业认证、快速学习成果和复杂下游分析能力三方面验证，为缩小企业技能差距提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: By 2030, 59 of every 100 workers will need reskilling or upskilling, yet the average time to close an enterprise skills gap grew from roughly 3 days in 2014 to 36 days in 2018. Most current frameworks accelerate single stages of upskilling programs and generally lack industry validation. We present an end-to-end framework that applies AI acceleration across five stages of knowledge acquisition, content development, content review and verification, teaching, and assessment development; with a strong focus on both production and learning efficiency. Three strong external signals validates the framework: the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy reviewed and approved an upskilling program built on the framework for continuing-professional-education credits; 3 learners followed the program and passed the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in a significantly short amount of time, with 14 more in progress; the program's knowledge base supports complex downstream analysis such as the production of a robust 1,267 risk item dataset for managing multi-agent AI system risks.

</details>


### [114] [Earthquaker-AI: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework with Rubric-Based Assessment for Primary School Earthquake Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14046)
*Xanthi Kokkinou,Chaido Mizeli,Nafsika Koulaxidou,Marina Delianidi,Konstantinos Diamantaras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Earthquaker-AI 是一个混合式教育框架，将基于检索增强生成（RAG）的对话式AI助手与Lego WeDo2机器人项目结合，旨在提升小学生的地震防灾意识和应对能力。系统通过机器人模拟地震响应，AI助手提供基于量规的反馈，并按照年级分阶段推进学习轨迹（从选择题到书面表达）。实验评估显示高准确率和低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STEM教育中的地震防灾项目多停留在机械模拟层面，缺乏认知和元认知层面的引导。作者希望将动手操作的机器人活动与AI对话系统结合，帮助学生不仅知道"怎么做"，还能理解"为什么这样做"，培养自我调节学习和危机状态下的冷静应对能力。

Method: 混合式教育框架，融合三部分：(1) Lego WeDo2机器人组件，模拟地震响应，通过传感器和制动器让学生直观感知防护动作；(2) RAG驱动的对话式AI助手，语义匹配学生查询与官方安全指南，生成准确响应；(3) 渐进式学习轨迹：低年级用二维量规评估选择题（识别安全动作），中年级用三维量规评估选择题（识别动作序列），高年级用四维量规评估书面表达（包含表达清晰度）。

Result: 实验评估显示系统具有高groundedness（基于事实的支撑性）和高准确性，低幻觉率。

Conclusion: Earthquaker-AI将动手操作、信息加工和反思实践有机结合，结合机器人、量规和AI能够促进技术素养、自我调节能力和数字系统的负责任使用，有助于培养早期危机管理技能。

Abstract: This paper presents Earthquaker-AI, a hybrid educational framework building upon a previously implemented educational robotics project by integrating a conversational AI assistant based on Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It aims to enhance earthquake preparedness and conscious action among primary-school students. The system extends the award-winning STEM project Earthquaker moving from mechanical simulation with Lego WeDo2 to cognitive and metacognitive processing. The robotics component uses Lego WeDo2 automation to simulate seismic response, letting students interact with sensors and actuators as tangible representations of protective actions. The assistant operates as a guided learning mechanism aligning student responses with safety guidelines, while providing rubric-based verbal feedback that supports self-regulated learning and calmness under emergency conditions. Earthquaker-AI follows a progressive learning trajectory aligned with cognitive development. In early grades, the focus is on basic recognition of safety actions through multiple-choice questions, assessed via a two-dimensional rubric. In middle grades, students identify correct action sequences through multiple-choice questions, evaluated via a three-axis rubric. In upper grades, the approach shifts to verbal production, requiring short written responses assessed via a four-dimensional rubric that includes clarity of expression. The dialogic module uses RAG to match student queries semantically with official guidelines, generating safe, accurate responses. Experimental evaluation shows high groundedness and accuracy, with a low hallucination rate. Overall, Earthquaker-AI combines hands-on engagement, information processing, and reflective practice. Combining robotics, rubrics, and AI promotes technological literacy, self-regulation, and responsible use of digital systems, contributing to early crisis-management skills.

</details>


### [115] [Deep Interaction: An Efficient Human-AI Interaction Method for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14049)
*Hefeng Zhou,Jinxuan Zhang,Jiong Lou,Yuxin Liu,Chaochao Lu,Jingjing Qu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Deep Interaction方法，允许用户直接编辑LLM的CoT推理错误部分，通过精炼编辑后的推理路径为蒸馏提示来引导模型沿正确路径推理，在STEM任务上纠正成功率提升25%以上，token使用减少约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理中，当模型出错时，传统交互方式要么重新生成可能再次出错的回复，要么用户费力标记错误步骤后模型仍可能重复类似错误，缺乏高效的错误纠正机制。

Method: 提出Deep Interaction机制：允许用户直接编辑原始CoT响应中的错误部分，保留正确推理步骤；将编辑后的CoT精炼为蒸馏提示（distilled prompt），用于引导LLM沿纠正后的推理路径继续生成。

Result: 在STEM任务推理上，相比基线方法，纠正成功率提升超过25%，token使用量减少约40%。

Conclusion: Deep Interaction通过直接编辑原响应而非重新生成，实现了更高效、更精准的推理错误纠正，同时大幅降低了计算开销。

Abstract: The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, when errors occur, current interaction approaches typically involve re-generating another response that may make mistakes again, or users laboriously flag the faulty step in follow-up turns that may get responses <You are right, I made a mistake here> followed by similar errors recurring. To address this issue, we propose an efficient human intervention mechanism for precisely correcting reasoning errors in LLMs, termed Deep Interaction. Our approach enables direct editing of the original response, allowing erroneous parts to be corrected while preserving accurate reasoning steps. We refine the edited CoT into a distilled prompt, which then steers the LLM along the corrected reasoning path. Experimental results show that our method achieves over a 25% improvement in correction success rate and reduces token usage by approximately 40% on STEM tasks reasoning compared to baseline approaches.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Overcoming slow Kolmogorov width decay in parametric optimal control via neural network surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13226)
*Hendrik Kleikamp,Martin Lazar,Juan Ricardo Muñoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化线性二次最优控制问题，提出基于U-Net的非线性代理模型来逼近最优终时伴随状态，解决了传统线性降阶模型因Kolmogorov宽度衰减慢而效率低下的问题，并通过数值实验证明U-Net在精度和训练效率上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性降阶模型在处理参数化最优控制问题时，由于终时伴随状态流形的Kolmogorov宽度衰减缓慢，需要很大的降阶空间才能保证精度，导致计算效率低下。作者希望克服这一局限性，寻找更高效的替代方案。

Method: 使用U-Net（一种非线性神经网络架构）作为代理模型，将参数场映射到近似终时伴随状态；同时采用合适的后验误差估计器对U-Net逼近结果进行验证和认证。

Result: 通过两个大规模数值实验，U-Net代理模型在所有对比方法（包括多种线性和非线性方法）中持续达到最高精度，且所需训练样本显著更少。

Conclusion: U-Net非线性代理模型能有效克服线性降阶模型在参数化最优控制问题中的局限性，在保证高精度的同时提高了训练效率，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper we deal with parametric, linear-quadratic optimal control problems in which the solution can be uniquely characterized by the optimal final time adjoint state. As a motivating example, we establish theoretical results showing that for distributed control of the heat equation, the manifold of final time adjoints over the parameter space exhibits a slow decay of its Kolmogorov width if this was already the case for the parameter-dependent target states. Traditional linear reduced-order models would thus require a large reduced space in order to guarantee a sufficient accuracy, making them inefficient in this application. To overcome the limitation of linear models, we discuss a nonlinear surrogate based on U-Nets that maps parametric fields to approximate final time adjoints. We show that a suitable a posteriori error estimator remains applicable to the U-Net approximation and can be used to certify the surrogate results. Through two extensive numerical experiments, we show the potential of the U-Net surrogate and compare it with several linear and nonlinear methods from the literature. The results show that the U-Net consistently achieves the highest accuracy among the methods considered while requiring significantly fewer training samples.

</details>


### [117] [Design of Carbon Capture Processes Under Part-load Operating Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13232)
*David Y. Shu,Boxun Huang,Yurim Kim,Randall Field,Rahul Gandhi,Sungho Shin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机优化的碳捕集过程设计方法，通过数据驱动的趋近平衡模型降低计算复杂度，并考虑燃煤电厂部分负荷运行造成的烟气条件变化。研究表明，在设计阶段考虑变负荷工况可降低设备尺寸和总成本6-9%，同时碳捕集总成本降低0.7-1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶剂法碳捕集过程设计通常基于单一运行点优化，但调峰电厂在实际运行中负荷变化导致烟气条件波动，现有方法未能充分考虑这一变异性。为降低多运行点优化带来的高计算复杂度，需要开发更高效的优化设计方法。

Method: 采用随机优化方法，利用数据驱动的趋近平衡模型（Approach-to-Equilibrium model）对吸收和解吸过程建模，以降低计算复杂度；使用代表性燃煤电厂的部分负荷运行数据表征可变烟气条件。

Result: 考虑烟气条件变异性后，设备尺寸和总工厂成本降低6-9%，但运行成本有所增加；碳捕集总成本降低0.7-1.7%。

Conclusion: 由于碳捕集过程属于资本密集型，烟气条件的变异性应在设计阶段予以充分考虑，尤其是对于需要负荷跟踪的电厂。

Abstract: Solvent-based carbon capture can reduce CO2 emissions resulting from a continued reliance on fossil power plants for firm power. These capture processes remove CO2 from flue gases via a solvent. Careful design via process systems optimization can limit the overall cost of carbon capture, which is both capital- and energy intensive. As dispatchable power plants operate to meet varying load demand, the design process needs to account for varying operating points. However, optimizing the design over multiple operating points yields high computational complexity, which is why designs are often based on a single operating point in practice. Here, we identify optimal carbon capture process designs via stochastic optimization, reducing computational complexity through a data-driven approach-to-equilibrium model of the absorption and desorption processes. We represent variable flue gas conditions based on part-load operation data of a representative coal power plant. Accounting for this variability in the design substantially reduces equipment size and total plant cost by 6-9 % at the expense higher operating costs, yielding a reduction in total cost of carbon capture by 0.7-1.7 %. Given the capital intensity of carbon capture, variability of flue gas conditions therefore should be considered at the design stage, particularly if capture is deployed on plants subject to load following.

</details>


### [118] [An ALNS Heuristic for Large-Scale Line Planning with Mode Choice and Line Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13263)
*Siv Marie Cartland Hansen,Richard Martin Lusby,Rowan Hoogervorst,Otto Anker Nielsen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应大邻域搜索（ALNS）算法，用于解决考虑内生需求的大规模公共交通线路规划问题。该算法在丹麦欧登塞的真实交通网络（约1800个OD对）上进行了验证，能够同时优化线路、发车频率，并考虑乘客模式选择、乘客分配和车辆容量。优化后的网络将平均发车间隔从41分钟缩短至6.8-13分钟，显著提升了公共交通乘客量。研究还表明，网络设计对乘客行为假设高度敏感，强调了校准需求模型的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共交通线路规划中需求响应性是一个重要考量因素，因为网络设计和服务质量会影响乘客需求。然而，考虑这种交互作用使得原本就具有挑战性的组合优化问题更加复杂。现有方法难以有效处理大规模场景下的线路规划与内生需求的联合优化问题。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的自适应大邻域搜索（ALNS）算法，动态生成候选线路，通过嵌入式的乘客分配与需求估计评估过程以及专用的频率优化局部搜索，联合优化线路和发车频率，同时考虑乘客模式选择、乘客分配和车辆容量约束。

Result: 在丹麦欧登塞约1800个OD对的真实交通网络上验证了算法的适用性。优化后网络将资源集中在更少但频率更高的线路上，平均发车间隔从约41分钟降至6.8-13分钟，公共交通乘客量显著增加。同时，网络设计对乘客行为假设高度敏感。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ALNS算法能够有效解决大规模、考虑内生需求的公共交通线路规划问题。研究结果强调了在将需求响应性纳入线路规划时，使用经过仔细校准的需求模型的重要性。

Abstract: Demand responsiveness is an important consideration in public transport line planning, as network design and service quality influence passenger demand. However, accounting for this interaction further complicates an already challenging combinatorial optimization problem. To address this challenge, we propose a scalable Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search (ALNS) algorithm for large-scale line planning with endogenous demand. The algorithm jointly optimizes lines and frequencies while accounting for passenger mode choice, passenger assignment, and vehicle capacities. Candidate lines are generated dynamically throughout the search, and solutions are evaluated using an embedded evaluation procedure for passenger assignment and demand estimation, together with a dedicated local search procedure for frequency optimization. The proposed methodology is evaluated on the public transport network of Odense, Denmark, comprising approximately 1,800 origin-destination pairs. Computational results demonstrate the applicability of the approach to realistic, large-scale instances. The optimized networks concentrate resources on fewer, higher-frequency services, reducing average headways from approximately 41 minutes to 6.8-13 minutes while substantially increasing public transport ridership. Furthermore, the results show that network design is highly sensitive to assumptions regarding passenger behavior, highlighting the importance of carefully calibrated demand models when incorporating demand responsiveness into line planning.

</details>


### [119] [Model predictive control for laser thermal processing: operator learning, closed-loop validation, and out-of-distribution analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13289)
*Smajil Halilovic,Yuchen Zhang,Daniel M. Tartakovsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一种基于深度算子网络的替代模型控制管道，用于激光粉末床熔融过程中移动激光源的表面峰值温度调控。该替代模型比传统有限差分方法快千倍以上，并成功将路径角点欠预测误差从91 K降至1.4 K，实现了零违规的温度上限控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融等激光热加工需要对峰值表面温度进行严格调控，但热积累效应会导致温度超出工艺窗口并产生缺陷。高保真热模型虽能准确捕捉物理过程，但计算速度过慢，无法用于在线优化，因此需要开发快速、可微分且具有泛化能力的替代模型。

Method: 提出一种针对移动热源定制的多步深度算子网络，其分支子网络编码未来功率和轨迹序列，主干子网络编码当前峰值温度及未来激光位置温度，实现一次性五步预测。将替代模型作为光滑非线性规划嵌入到滚动时域模型预测控制中，使用CasADi/IPOPT求解。采用受控双集成数据设计来改进模型泛化能力。

Result: 替代模型前向传播比等效有限差分方法快千倍以上。两个离线误差几乎相同的替代模型在闭环中表现截然不同，表明聚合开环精度对于控制就绪性必要但不充分。受控双集成数据设计将91 K的路径角点欠预测失败降至1.4 K，采用13 K校准单边约束裕度后所有测试路径均实现零违规。

Conclusion: 开发并验证了完整的基于替代模型的控制管道，证明了替代模型的快速性和有效性。强调了离线精度不能保证闭环控制性能，提出了数据设计和约束裕度校准策略来确保控制可靠性。

Abstract: Laser-based thermal processing, such as laser powder bed fusion, requires tight regulation of the peak surface temperature: heat accumulates where the moving source re-enters previously heated material, driving the temperature out of its process window and causing defects. High-fidelity thermal models capture this physics but are too slow for online optimization, which motivates fast, differentiable, and generalizable surrogates. We develop and validate a complete surrogate-based control pipeline that regulates the maximum surface temperature of a moving laser on a 304-stainless-steel substrate. We also determine conditions under which our surrogate can be trusted inside the control loop by probing its out-of-distribution limits. A key component of our surrogate is a multi-step deep operator network bespoke for moving sources: its branch subnetwork encodes the future power and trajectory (position and velocity) sequence, while its trunk encodes the current peak temperature and the temperature at the future laser locations, yielding a one-shot five-step prediction. By way of illustration, we use this surrogate as a smooth (algebraic-rectifier) nonlinear program inside a receding-horizon model predictive controller solved in CasADi/IPOPT. The surrogate forward pass is over thousand times faster than the equivalent finite-difference steps. We show that aggregate open-loop accuracy is necessary but not sufficient for control-readiness: two surrogates with near-identical offline error behave drastically differently in closed loop. A controlled two-ensemble data design reduces a 91 K path-corner underprediction failure to 1.4 K, and a calibrated one-sided constraint margin of 13 K yields zero violations of the true upper bound on all tested paths.

</details>


### [120] [State constrained convex Nash equilibrium problems coupled with linear hyperbolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13299)
*Marcelo Bongarti,Michael Hintermüller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了与双曲型偏微分方程耦合的状态约束凸广义纳什均衡问题的解的存在性，并给出了线性双曲PDE类的一阶最优性条件，应用于波动方程、广告动力学和网络上的线性化等温欧拉系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲型PDE耦合的GNEP因策略-状态映射的紧致性问题导致集值约束映射正则性不足，阻碍了存在性理论的发展；椭圆和抛物型PDE情形已有研究，但双曲情形尚未解决。

Method: 通过处理双曲型PDE带来的紧凑性困难，建立状态约束凸GNEP的均衡存在性理论；针对一类一般线性双曲PDE推导一阶最优性条件。

Result: 成功建立了双曲型PDE耦合的状态约束凸GNEP的均衡存在性理论，并给出了线性双曲PDE类的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 本文填补了双曲型PDE耦合GNEP存在性理论的空白，所得一阶最优性条件可应用于波动方程、广告动力学和网络上的线性化等温欧拉系统等实际问题。

Abstract: We study the existence of equilibria for state constrained, convex generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) coupled with hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs). Analogous problems have been addressed for state constrained GNEPs coupled with elliptic and parabolic PDEs, respectively, but the problematic regularity of the set-valued constraint maps has been a barrier for development of an existence theory in the hyperbolic case. This is mainly due to compactness issues with the strategy-to-state maps. Beyond existence, we also provide first-order optimality conditions for a class of fairly general linear hyperbolic PDEs and show its relevance for applications such as the wave equation, advertising dynamics, and the linearized isothermal Euler system on networks.

</details>


### [121] [Closing the Oracle-Complexity Gap in Derivative-Free Convex Optimization: A Near-Quadratic Lower Bound from Exact Function Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13335)
*Phillip Kerger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅使用精确函数值最小化凸Lipschitz函数的确定性查询复杂度，在d维欧几里得球上。对于精度Θ(d^{-1/2})，此前下界为Ω(d)，上界为O(d² log² d)。本文给出了Ω(d²/log(d+1))的下界，缩小了自1996年以来的差距至多对数因子。并将结果推广到混合整数设置：具有d个连续变量和n个离散变量的混合整数凸优化需要Ω̃(d²·2ⁿ)次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合自1996年以来仅使用函数值的凸优化查询复杂度上下界之间的差距，并推广到混合整数优化场景。

Method: 通过构造对抗性论证或信息论下界技术，证明确定性算法在仅使用零阶（函数值）信息时需要Ω(d²/log d)次查询才能达到精度Θ(d^{-1/2})；并通过组合扩展，将该下界提升到混合整数设置。

Result: 在d维欧几里得球上，精度Θ(d^{-1/2})时，零阶凸优化的确定性查询复杂度下界为Ω(d²/log(d+1))；混合整数设置下为Ω̃(d²·2ⁿ)。

Conclusion: 闭合了下界Ω(d)与上界O(d² log² d)之间的差距（至多对数因子），并证明了混合整数凸优化在零阶信息下的指数级下界。

Abstract: We study the deterministic query complexity of minimizing a convex Lipschitz function over a $d$-dimensional Euclidean ball using only exact function values. At accuracy $Θ(d^{-1/2})$, the previously applicable lower bound was $Ω(d)$, inherited from the stronger full first-order oracle, while an upper bound from Protasov's value-only method requires $O(d^2\log^2 d)$ evaluations. By providing a lower bound of $Ω(\,\frac{d^2}{\log(d+1)})$ on the oracle complexity in this setting, we thereby close this gap dating back to 1996, up to polylogarithmic factors. Furthermore, we are able to lift this result to the mixed-integer setting: Mixed-integer convex optimization with $d$ continuous and $n$ discrete variables using function values requires $\tildeΩ(d^2\cdot 2^n)$ queries.

</details>


### [122] [Learned Pairwise Deep Dual-Optimal Inequalities for Stabilizing Column Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13373)
*Zhengzhong Ricky You,Bo Tang,Haoran Liu,Baichuan Mo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出L-PDDOI框架，通过机器学习预测对偶变量间的偏序关系并转化为可行不等式，加速列生成收敛。在CVRP和VRPTW上，直接部署减少89.7%和93.9%的几何平均根CG时间，同时仅损失1.3%和0.5%的界；恢复过程保证无界损失下分别减少54.8%和83.1%的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列生成中不稳定的对偶解会显著降低收敛速度，现有深度对偶最优不等式虽能缓解此问题，但其构造依赖问题特定的交换论证，难以推广到带容量约束、时间窗等资源约束的路径问题。

Method: 提出L-PDDOI框架：先采样最优对偶解，选取在足够大公共子集上同时成立的成对序关系作为训练标签；用分类器为候选关系评分；通过基于图的后处理过滤和压缩候选集；引入恢复过程选择性松弛所学不等式，在恢复基线CG界时提供证书。

Result: 在CVRP和VRPTW主测试集上，直接部署L-PDDOI分别减少89.7%和93.9%的几何平均根CG时间，界损失仅1.3%和0.5%。恢复过程在保证不损失CG界的前提下，分别减少54.8%和83.1%的时间。

Conclusion: L-PDDOI是一种通用、有效的学习框架，能自动生成深度对偶最优不等式以加速列生成，且恢复过程可消除界损失风险，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Column generation (CG) is central to many large-scale optimization algorithms, including branch-price-and-cut methods for vehicle routing problems, but unstable dual solutions can substantially slow its convergence. Existing deep dual-optimal inequalities can reduce this instability by restricting the dual space. Their construction, however, typically relies on problem-specific exchange arguments that are difficult to establish for routing problems with capacity limits, time windows, and other resource constraints. We introduce learned pairwise deep dual-optimal inequalities (L-PDDOIs), a learning framework that predicts pairwise orderings between dual variables and incorporates their primal counterparts directly into the master problem. To construct training labels, the framework samples optimal dual solutions and selects pairwise order relations that hold simultaneously on a sufficiently large common subset of the samples. A classifier then assigns a score to each candidate relation. Because conflicts and redundancies among the predicted relations can impair performance, graph-based postprocessing filters and compresses the candidate set before deployment. We further introduce a recovery procedure that selectively relaxes learned inequalities and provides a certificate when the baseline CG bound has been restored. On the main test sets for the capacitated vehicle routing problem and the vehicle routing problem with time windows, direct deployment of L-PDDOIs reduces the geometric mean root CG time by 89.7% and 93.9%, respectively, while incurring mean bound losses of only 1.3% and 0.5%. The recovery procedure retains corresponding time reductions of 54.8% and 83.1%, respectively, while guaranteeing no loss in the CG bound.

</details>


### [123] [Hager-Zhang Conjugate Gradient Method for Set Optimization with Set-Valued Objective Map of Finite Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13383)
*Ravi Raushan,Debdas Ghosh,Zai-Yun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解集优化问题的非线性Hager-Zhang共轭梯度法，该方法不依赖有限生成锥或正则性条件，通过Wolfe线搜索和标量参数定义了下降方向，理论证明了全局收敛性，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集优化方法往往对生成锥的有限生成性或最优解处的正则性条件有较强依赖，限制了其适用范围。本文旨在提出一种不受这些限制的非线性共轭梯度方法，同时适用于集优化和向量优化问题。

Method: 采用Drummond-Svaiter标量化函数讨论Wolfe线搜索条件，证明沿下降方向存在满足该条件的步长；引入Hager-Zhang标量共轭参数构造搜索方向，证明该方向为下降方向；建立Zoutendijk-like条件以证明全局收敛性。

Result: 证明了所提方法的良定性，方向是下降方向；建立了Zoutendijk-like条件并渐近地证明了全局收敛性；数值实验表明该方法在实际问题中表现良好有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的非线性Hager-Zhang共轭梯度法在无需有限生成锥或正则性假设下实现了集优化问题的求解，具有理论上的收敛保障和实际应用的有效性。

Abstract: This work introduces a nonlinear Hager-Zhang conjugate gradient method for solving set optimization problems. The objective function under consideration is defined by a finite collection of continuously differentiable functions. Notably, the proposed approach imposes restrictions neither on the existence of a finite generator of the ordering cone nor on any regularity condition at the optimal solution. As a result, the proposed method holds considerable significance for both set optimization and vector optimization problems, with the latter serving as a special case of the former. The study begins by discussing Wolfe line search conditions using Drummond-Svaiter scalarization function. Thereafter, we establish the existence of a step length satisfying the Wolfe line search conditions along a descent direction. The Hager-Zhang scalar conjugate parameter is introduced to derive the search direction for the proposed method. It is established that the direction generated by the proposed method is a descent direction. The well-definedness of the proposed method is given. Furthermore, we discuss some important results and a Zoutendijk-like condition to ensure global convergence. Subsequently, the global convergence of the proposed method is established in an asymptotic manner. Finally, numerical experiments on various test problems validate the practical performance and effectiveness of the proposed technique.

</details>


### [124] [Norm-Cone Conjugation and Fenchel-Type Duality Beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13495)
*Fernando García-Castaño,Miguel Ángel Melguizo-Padial*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范数锥的共轭方案，将经典Fenchel共轭中的仿射支撑推广为范数锥支撑，建立了超越凸性的非线性共轭框架，并发展了相应的对偶理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破经典Fenchel共轭对凸性的依赖，建立适用于非凸函数的共轭与对偶理论框架。

Method: 用形如 \(x\mapsto r-\alpha\|x-x_0\|\) 的平移范数锥替换仿射函数作为支撑，构造新的共轭变换（索引为斜率和中心），定义范数锥次微分，并基于扰动变量发展部分范数锥共轭的对偶理论。

Result: 建立了Fenchel-Young型不等式，引入了可容许斜率和高度概念，刻画了精确双共轭的范数锥支撑条件；弱对偶性无需凸性假设，强对偶性依赖于度量下界条件（一致下Lipschitz估计和下calmness）。

Conclusion: 范数锥共轭为非凸优化提供了一种新的对偶分析工具，拓展了共轭对偶理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We introduce a norm-cone conjugation scheme for extended-real-valued functions on normed spaces. The construction replaces affine minorants by translated norm-cones of the form \(x\mapsto r-α\|x-x_0\|\), with \(α\ge0\), and establishes a nonlinear conjugation framework underlying a Fenchel-type duality theory beyond convexity. The resulting conjugate is indexed by slopes and centres, and the associated biconjugate is the supremum of all norm-cone minorants lying below the function. We prove Fenchel--Young type inequalities, introduce admissible slopes and admissible heights, and characterize exact biconjugation in terms of norm-cone supportability. We also define a norm-cone subdifferential and relate it to exact support and biconjugation. Finally, we develop an abstract perturbation duality theory based on partial norm-cone conjugation in the perturbation variable. Weak duality holds without convexity assumptions, while strong duality follows from metric lower-bound conditions, including uniform lower Lipschitz estimates and lower calmness of the value function.

</details>


### [125] [Energy decay and weak observability for some evolution systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13502)
*Kaïs Ammari,Faouzi Triki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在抽象泛函框架下证明了弱可观性与能量衰减在适当假设下是等价的，为耗散系统提供了统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究演化方程及其长时间行为，探究弱可观性与能量衰减之间的关系。

Method: 基于弱可观性理论的最新进展（特别是\cite{AT1}中的新技术），通过量化弱可观性不等式，弥合定性衰减性质与谱估计之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在适当假设下，弱可观性与能量衰减不仅是紧密相关的，而且是等价的。

Conclusion: 为抽象动力系统稳定性和控制提供了统一视角，建立了定性衰减性质与谱估计之间的联系。

Abstract: In an abstract functional framework, we investigate the equivalence between two fundamental properties: weak observability and energy decay associated with certain classes of dissipative operators. This analysis is motivated by the study of evolution equations and their long-time behavior. Our main result establishes that under appropriate assumptions, these two notions are not only closely related but in fact equivalent. The proof relies on recent advances in the theory of weak observability, particularly those presented in \cite{AT1}, where new techniques have been developed to quantify the weak observability inequalities. These tools allow us to bridge the gap between qualitative decay properties and spectral estimates, offering a unified perspective on stability and control in abstract dynamical systems.

</details>


### [126] [Infinite horizon stochastic optimal control with sign-changing discount factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13547)
*Charles Bertucci,Jean-Michel Lasry,Pierre-Louis Lions*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman 方程研究了一个无限时域随机最优控制问题，其特点是折现因子可正可负，取决于状态变量的取值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究折现因子随状态变化而可正可负的随机最优控制问题，现有理论对此类变号折现因子的情况尚缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 采用 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) 方程作为分析工具，处理无限时域下折现因子符号依赖于状态变量的随机最优控制问题。

Result: 给出了此类变号折现因子随机最优控制问题通过 HJB 方程求解的理论框架。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了随机最优控制理论中关于折现因子的假设条件，为更广泛的经济、金融等应用场景提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study an infinite horizon stochastic optimal control problem by means of the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. The problem we are studying has the particularity of having a discount factor which can take both signs, depending on the value of the state.

</details>


### [127] [The Value of Perfect Endpoint Forecasts for Offshore-Wind Thermal Firming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13627)
*Arash Khojaste,Geoffrey Pritchard,Golbon Zakeri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种仅基于当前和单个未来端点（而非完整路径）的短期风电出力预报对海上风电热备调峰成本的影响，发现端点预报越远（如6小时），节省的调峰成本反而越小（2.28% vs 1小时的8.07%），表明远期单端点信息对短期爬坡决策的可操作性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预报价值评估多关注精度，但忽略了运行模型中信息结构的可嵌套性（即较远预报是否包含较近预报的信息）。本文旨在研究一种非嵌套的、仅依赖当前和单个未来端点的预报信息结构对离岸风电热备调峰成本的影响，并揭示其与滚动路径预报不同的价值递减规律。

Method: 将问题建模为周期平稳马氏决策过程（MDP），利用ISO新英格兰负荷和离岸风电数据，通过分位数傅里叶回归提取状态；针对不同预报时长h=1,…,6，求解年度状态-动作频率线性规划（LP）来计算热备调峰成本。

Result: 1小时端点预报降低年度调峰成本8.07%，而6小时端点预报仅降低2.28%；端点预报的节费效益随预报时长增加而递减。

Conclusion: 仅凭单个远期端点预报对单步爬坡决策的指导价值有限，预报信息结构（端点 vs 路径）对运行决策价值有显著影响，且不否定滚动长期路径预报的潜在价值。

Abstract: Forecast value depends not only on accuracy but also on the information structure available to the operating model. We study a diagnostic current-endpoint-only forecast for offshore-wind thermal firming: at hour t, the controller observes the current net-demand state z_t and one perfect future target z_{t+h}, but not the intermediate path or earlier endpoint messages. Unlike rolling path forecasts, these endpoint information sets are not nested in h. We embed the signal in a cyclostationary MDP using quantile Fourier regression states estimated from ISO New England load and offshore wind data, and solve annual state-action-frequency LPs for h = 1, . . . , 6. A one-hour endpoint forecast reduces annual firming cost by 8.07%, while a six-hour endpoint reduces it by 2.28%. The decreasing profile shows that a single farther endpoint is less actionable for a one-step ramping decision, without implying that longer rolling forecasts are less valuable.

</details>


### [128] [Meeting Uncertain Threats with Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13648)
*Louis L Chen,Ang Xu,Roberto Szechtman,Chiwei Yan,Vince Vanterpool*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在不确定性和延迟观测条件下，使用有限效应器防御多枚来袭威胁的资源分配问题，建立了马尔可夫决策过程模型，并提出了公平分配和贪婪策略等简单高效的时间无关策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防空反导、海军力量保护和关键基础设施安全需要快速将稀缺效应器分配给多个来袭威胁，且中和结果不确定且仅在发射轮次后才能观测，亟需高效的分配策略。

Method: 将防御分配问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），指挥官每轮将固定效应器容量分配给异构威胁，考虑了三种操作目标（最小化期望清除时间、最大化截止时间前清除概率、最大化截止时间前有效分配）。研究了公平分配策略和基于威胁难度的贪婪策略。

Result: 公平分配在同质威胁或低容量交战中对所有三个目标都是最优的；当效应器容量与威胁数量至少线性增长时，其清除时间与最优策略的差距最多为常数轮；贪婪策略在异构实例中接近最优，公平分配在威胁难度未知时仍是原则性选择。

Conclusion: 公平分配是一种简单高效的防御资源分配策略，在多种场景下具有理论最优性或近似最优性，贪婪策略可进一步提升异构场景下的性能。

Abstract: Air and missile defense, naval force protection, and critical-infrastructure security require rapid allocation of scarce effectors against multiple incoming threats when neutralization is uncertain and observed only after a firing round. We formulate this defensive-allocation problem as a Markov decision process in which a commander assigns a fixed amount of effector capacity each round to heterogeneous threats under three operational objectives: minimizing expected threat-clearance time, maximizing the probability of clearance by a deadline, and maximizing effective assignments before a deadline. Rather than expensive full dynamic optimization, we study simple time-oblivious policies suited to fast implementation. Fair allocation, which ignores threat difficulty and spreads fire evenly, is highly effective: it is optimal for all three objectives under homogeneous threats or low-capacity engagements. Moreover, when effector capacity scales at least linearly with the number of threats, its threat-clearance time trails that of the optimal policy by at most a constant number of rounds. We further develop threat-difficulty-aware greedy policies for each objective, including a constant-factor guarantee for effective assignment maximization. Numerically, greedy policies are near-optimal across heterogeneous instances, while fair allocation remains a principled choice when threat difficulties are unknown.

</details>


### [129] [The problem of finding the global minimum of a polynomial on a coordinate parallelepiped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13658)
*V. N. Nefedov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变量消去算法（VEA）求解多项式全局优化问题的方法，适用于坐标平行六面体等简单结构集合上的多项式全局最小值求解，但仅对低维度和低次多项式具有实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决坐标平行六面体或简单结构集合上多项式全局最小值求解问题。

Method: 采用作者先前工作中描述的变量消去算法（Variable Elimination Algorithm, VEA）求解多项式优化问题。

Result: VEA在理论上适用于任意多项式优化问题，但实际实现中会面临大量高次多项式实根确定问题，仅对低维度和低次多项式具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 尽管VEA存在维度灾难和次数增长的实际限制，但对于某些实际相关的问题，该方法仍能成功求解。

Abstract: The paper addresses the problem of finding the global minimum of a polynomial on a coordinate parallelepiped or on certain other sets with a simple structure.
  To solve the problem, the Variable Elimination Algorithm for polynomial optimization problems described in the author's previous works is used.
  It should be noted that the proposed method can be of practical significance only for problems of low dimensionality and for polynomials of not very high degree.
  This is due to the fact that, when implementing the VEA, a large number of alternative problems may arise concerning the determination of real roots of polynomials, and these polynomials may have very high degrees.
  Although, in theory, the Variable Elimination Algorithm is applicable to any polynomial optimization problems, there are often practically relevant problems that can be successfully solved using the proposed method.

</details>


### [130] [Lifting-Free Quadratic Sum-Of-Squares Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13701)
*Gabriel F. Machado,Ross Drummond,Morgan Jones*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需lifting的QSOS优化正则化方法，通过添加SOS变量范数惩罚保持原始锥结构，实现闭式原始更新和Lipschitz光滑凹对偶，加速一阶方法高效求解，在约束回归中比SCS快40%，内存仅随等式约束数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Schur补和二阶锥lifting会扩大锥维度，导致内点法计算瓶颈，需在保持原始锥结构的同时提升求解效率。

Method: 引入lifting-free正则化，对SOS变量添加范数惩罚项，导出闭式原始更新和无约束凹对偶问题（Lipschitz连续梯度），采用加速一阶方法最大化对偶，并给出非渐近收敛性分析。

Result: 数值实验表明，在约束回归问题上，所提方法比SCS快40%，可处理比MOSEK更大规模的问题，内存仅随等式约束个数线性扩展。

Conclusion: lifting-free正则化有效避免了锥维度扩张，兼顾了计算效率与可扩展性，适用于大规模QSOS优化问题。

Abstract: Quadratic Sum-Of-Squares (QSOS) optimization problems appear in system identification and machine learning, but standard Schur-complement and second-order cone liftings enlarge conic dimensions and create computational bottlenecks for interior-point methods. This paper introduces a lifting-free regularization that preserves the original conic structure by adding a norm penalty to SOS variables, yielding closed-form primal updates and an unconstrained, concave dual with Lipschitz-continuous gradient. Accelerated first-order methods efficiently maximize this dual, and convergence analysis shows non-asymptotic recovery of the solution. Numerical experiments on constrained regression problems show the proposed method can be 40\% faster than existing solvers such as SCS and handle larger problems than MOSEK, with memory scaling only in the number of equality constraints.

</details>


### [131] [Polynomial iISS for a class of Timoshenko equations with external disturbances and infinite history memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13715)
*Xinyu Guo,Jun Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在积分输入到状态稳定性（iISS）框架下研究具有外部扰动和无限历史记忆的Timoshenko方程的鲁棒性，提出了仅基于状态范数的多项式iISS（PiISS）定义，并在Tolksdorf条件下推导了PiISS和指数iISS（EiISS）的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PiISS定义依赖状态图范数，与经典iISS概念不一致，需要为无穷维Timoshenko系统建立与状态范数一致的PiISS严格定义。

Method: 利用半群理论和椭圆方程理论建立适定性；通过构造适当的Lyapunov泛函并结合多种先验估计来评估PiISS和EiISS。

Result: 在Tolksdorf条件下推导了PiISS的充分条件，在更强条件下得到EiISS的充分条件，完整刻画了扰动和历史记忆对系统稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为Timoshenko方程在扰动和记忆效应下的稳定性分析提供了更一致且严谨的iISS理论框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates the robustness of Timoshenko equations subject to external disturbances and infinite history memory within the integral input-to-state stability (iISS) framework. While polynomial iISS (PiISS) is a powerful tool for characterizing the influence of these two factors, the existing definitions of PiISS rely on graph norms of the state, which are inconsistent with the classical iISS notion for infinite-dimensional systems. In this paper, we provide a rigorous PiISS definition for a class of Timoshenko equations solely via the state norm, and derive sufficient conditions for PiISS under the Tolksdorf condition on the relaxation function, as well as exponential iISS (EiISS) under a stronger condition. The well-posedness is established by using the semigroup and elliptic equation theories. The PiISS and EiISS are assessed by constructing appropriate Lyapunov functionals and employing various a priori estimates of solutions, thereby fully characterizing the impact of disturbances and history memory on system stability.

</details>


### [132] [Bayesian Risk Preference Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13810)
*Shutian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个信息设计框架，研究如何通过信息操纵决策者的风险偏好，从而影响其后续决策结果。发送者设计信息规则，接收者观察到系统状态后会更新其风险偏好（需保持时间一致性），发送者通过控制信念分布实现最优信息设计。文章刻画了最优条件，并以再保险设计为例展示了框架在风险管理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者的风险偏好并非固定不变，而是会随外部信息动态调整，进而影响其后续选择与结果。现有研究缺乏对如何主动设计信息来引导风险偏好变化的理论框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 建立了一个包含发送者（信息设计者）和接收者（决策者）的说服博弈模型。接收者以一致风险测度表示初始风险偏好，在观察到发送者设计的信息规则所生成的系统状态后，更新其偏好。偏好更新须满足状态实现前后风险评价的时间一致性。通过分析后验信念的诱导分布，刻画发送者的最优信息设计。

Result: 刻画了发送者在多种情境下通过信息设计获益的条件；通过再保险设计应用，展示了该框架在风险管理中的实用潜力。

Conclusion: 信息设计可以有效引导决策者的风险偏好调整，从而影响其风险评价和决策结果。该框架为风险管理实践（如再保险设计）提供了新的理论工具和视角。

Abstract: A decision-maker's risk preference is inherently unstable and may adjust in response to external information, shaping subsequent choices and outcomes. This paper develops a persuasion framework to study how information can be designed to steer risk preferences and decision results. In our model, a receiver starts with an initial risk preference represented by a coherent risk measure and revises it after observing a system state generated by an information rule claimed by a sender. The revision must preserve time consistency of risk evaluations before and after the state realization. We characterize the sender's optimal information design by analyzing the induced distribution of posterior beliefs over states. Each belief leads to specific preference revisions and corresponding conditional risk assessments. We identify conditions under which information design benefits the sender across several settings and illustrate the framework's potential in risk management through an application to reinsurance design.

</details>


### [133] [Associated gradients: connection to conservative fields and application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13973)
*Cheik Traoré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了局部Lipschitz分段光滑函数表示对应的梯度是保守场的选取，该梯度集合沿Lipschitz曲线满足链式法则，从而统一了非光滑自动微分的两个理论框架，并证明了新一类路径可微函数。在算法上，基于有界性假设，证明了使用关联梯度的随机次梯度方法迭代序列收敛到保守和Clarke临界点，以及函数值的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合非光滑自动微分中两种理论框架之间的鸿沟，为分段光滑函数的梯度表示提供严格的数学基础，统一保守场理论与Clarke次微分理论。

Method: 通过证明局部Lipschitz分段光滑函数表示关联的梯度集合是保守场的选取，验证其沿Lipschitz曲线满足链式法则；利用Clarke次微分作为该集合凸包子集的性质，建立两者之间的包含关系；基于有界性假设，分析随机次梯度方法的收敛性质。

Result: 1) 分段光滑函数表示关联的梯度构成的集合具有链式法则性质，因而是保守场的选取；2) Clarke次微分也继承该性质；3) 证明了一类新的路径可微函数；4) 随机次梯度方法在算法上收敛到保守临界点和Clarke临界点，且函数值收敛。

Conclusion: 成功统一了非光滑自动微分中两个重要的理论框架，为使用关联梯度驱动优化算法提供了理论保证，拓展了路径可微函数的类别。

Abstract: In this paper, we demonstrate that the gradient associated to a representation of a locally Lipschitz, piecewise-smooth function is a selection of a conservative field. Indeed, we prove that a well-defined set, containing these associated gradients, has the chain rule property along Lipschitz curves. Consequently, the Clarke subdifferential, as a subset of its convex hull, also inherits this property for these functions. Ultimately, this work reconciles two theoretical frameworks that address notably nonsmooth automatic differentiation. As a byproduct, it also proves a new class of path differentiable functions. From an algorithmic perspective, under a boundedness assumption, we provide the subsequence convergence of the iterates to both conservative and Clarke critical points, as well as the convergence of the function values, for the stochastic subgradient method, where associated gradients are used to drive the dynamic.

</details>
